\displaystyle\quad\text{for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\text{ and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]

\displaystyle 8v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,N-3)-65v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,N-3)+57v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,N-3)>0,\quad\text{for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)

(115)

\displaystyle\quad\text{and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}],

\displaystyle-8v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,N-3)-v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,N-3)-48v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,N-2)+57v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,N-3)>0,

(117)

\displaystyle\quad\text{for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\text{ and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}],

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,j)

(118)

\displaystyle\quad+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,j+1)>0,\quad j=2,\ldots,N-4

\displaystyle\quad\text{for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\quad\text{and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}],

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,j)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,j+1)>0,

(119)

\displaystyle\quad j=2,\ldots,N-4,\text{ for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\text{ and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]

\displaystyle 8v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j)+16v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,j+1)-15v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,j)+48v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,j+1)-57v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,j)>0,

(120)

\displaystyle\quad j=2,\ldots,N-4,\text{ for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\quad\text{and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}],

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j+1)>0,\quad\lambda\in(0,1),\quad i,j=2,\ldots,N-4,

(121)

\displaystyle\quad\text{for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\text{ and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}],

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j+1)>0,\quad\lambda\in(0,1),\quad i,j=2,\ldots,N-4,

(122)

\displaystyle\quad\text{for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\text{ and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}],

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j+2)>0,

(123)

\displaystyle\quad i,j=2,\ldots,N-4\text{ for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\quad\text{and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i,j+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+1,j+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(i+2,j)>0,

(124)

\displaystyle\quad i,j=2,\ldots,N-4,\text{ for }k=1,\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)\quad\text{and }k=2,\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}].

We start by showing that
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j):=v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)
, for all
j=2,\ldots,N-2
. This statement is trivial for
j=N-4,N-3,N-2,N
, by the fact that
d^{1}(N,j)=d^{2}(N,j)
for these values of
j
and the fact that rectangles cannot shrink. Let
v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j):=v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)
for
j=N-4,N-3,N-2,N
. Using expression (
96
) and recalling that
\lambda_{c}=15/17
, we compute
v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-4)=19550/3551
and
v_{\lambda_{c}}(NN-3)=23460/3551
. We now use a backward induction argument to show that
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)
holds for
j=2,\ldots,N-5
. First of all, we verify its validity for
j=N-5
. By expression (
96
), we have

v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-5)=\dfrac{2\lambda_{c}}{3-\lambda_{c}}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-4)=\dfrac{48875}{10653}.

By expression (
97
), we obtain

v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-5)=\dfrac{7\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-4)+\dfrac{5\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-3)=\dfrac{48875}{10653}.

Thus, we have

v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,N-5)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,N-5)=\dfrac{48875}{10653}:=v_{\lambda^{c}}(N,N-5).

Similarly, we find

v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-6)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,N-6)=\dfrac{244375}{63918}:=v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,N-6).

Now, suppose that
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j):=v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)
for all
j\geq n+1
,
n=2,\ldots,N-7
. We proceed to show that this implies
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n):=v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n)
.
Using expression (
96
) and the induction hypothesis, we obtain

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{2\lambda_{c}}{3-\lambda_{c}}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{7\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+1)+\left(\dfrac{2\lambda_{c}}{3-\lambda_{c}}-\dfrac{7\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}\right)\dfrac{2\lambda_{c}}{3-\lambda_{c}}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+2)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{7\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda^{c})}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+1)+\dfrac{2\lambda_{c}^{2}(33-23\lambda_{c})}{3(3-\lambda_{c})^{2}(9-5\lambda_{c})}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+2)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{7\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+1)+\dfrac{5\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n+2),

where we used the fact that

\dfrac{2\lambda_{c}^{2}(33-23\lambda_{c})}{3(3-\lambda_{c})^{2}(9-5\lambda_{c})}=\dfrac{25}{78}=\dfrac{5\lambda_{c}}{3(9-5\lambda_{c})}.

Thus, by expression (
97
) and the induction hypothesis, we have
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,n)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,n):=v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,n)
. It follows that
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,j)=v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda^{c}}(N,j):=v_{\lambda_{c}}(N,j)
for all
j=2,\ldots,N-2
. Note that this immediately implies the validity of equation (
107
) above.

We now turn our attention to the remaining inequalities listed above. Inequalities (
103
), (
104
), (
108
), (
109
), (
110
), (
111
), (
115
) and (
116
) can be verified easily using the explicit expressions for the value functions collected in Table
14
. We now proceed to prove the remaining inequalities by means of induction over the size of the rectangle.

Inequality (
105
)

First, we verify the validity of inequality (
105
) for
j=N-5
using the explicit expressions for
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,N-5)
,
v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,N-4)
and
v^{\pi}_{\lambda}(N,N-3)
,
\lambda\in(\lambda_{c},1)
recorded in Table
14
. Now, assume that it holds for
j=n+1
for some
n=2,\ldots,N-6
. Using expression (
96
), we obtain for
j=n
:

\displaystyle-6v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n)+11v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{2\lambda}{3-\lambda}\Big(-6v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+11v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)-5v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+3)\Big).

From the induction hypothesis, it now follows that

-6v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n)+11v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)>0.

Thus, inequality (
105
) holds for all
j=2,\ldots,N-5
.

Inequality (
106
)

We first verify the correctness of inequality (
106
) for
j=N-5
and
j=N-6
using the explicit expressions collected in Table
14
. Suppose now that it is valid for all
j\in\{n+1,\ldots,N-5\}
for some
n=2,\ldots,N-7
. Using expression (
97
), we obtain

\displaystyle 6v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n)-11v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+5v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)

\displaystyle=6\left(\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)\right)

\displaystyle-11\left(\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+3)\right)

\displaystyle+5\left(\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+3)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+4)\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}\Big(6v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)-11v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)+5v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+3)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}\Big(6v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)-11v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+3)+5v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+4)\Big).

Invoking the induction hypothesis now yields

6v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n)-11v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+5v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)>0.

It follows that inequality (
106
) holds for all
j=2,\ldots,N-5
.

Inequality (
112
)

We start by verifying the validity of inequality (
112
) for
j=N-4
using the explicit expressions for the value functions in Table
14
. Then, we assume that it holds for
j=n+1
for some

and show that its validity carries over to
j=n
. Expression (
100
) implies

\displaystyle 6v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n)-40v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n)+25v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{72}{12-5\lambda}\left(\dfrac{\lambda}{8}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n)+\dfrac{25\lambda}{72}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\dfrac{480}{12-5\lambda}\left(\dfrac{\lambda}{8}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+\dfrac{25\lambda}{72}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+2)\right)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n)+25v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{4\lambda}{3(12-5\lambda)}\Big(6v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)-40v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+2)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)+25v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{36(3-\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n)+\dfrac{4(75-43\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+1)-\dfrac{200\lambda}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,n+2),

for

,

From the induction hypothesis, it follows that it suffices to show that

(125)

for all

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{27(20-9\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}\left(\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}\left(-\dfrac{27(20-9\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)+\dfrac{3(100-\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}\left(-\dfrac{27(20-9\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)+\dfrac{3(100-\lambda)}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+3)\right).

By the induction hypothesis, it now follows that

. This implies that expression (

) holds for all

, for
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
as well. Thus, we can conclude that

for

,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. This establishes the validity of inequality (

) for all

, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
.

\displaystyle-\dfrac{785\lambda^{2}}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)-\dfrac{125\lambda^{2}}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda(2025-1534\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)-\dfrac{785\lambda^{2}}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{125\lambda^{2}}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+5)\Bigg]\geq 0.

\displaystyle=\dfrac{8\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\dfrac{12}{12-5\lambda}\left[\dfrac{\lambda}{8}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell})+\dfrac{25\lambda}{72}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,\tilde{\ell}+1)\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\dfrac{55\lambda}{9-4\lambda}-\dfrac{20\lambda}{12-5\lambda}\right]\dfrac{12}{12-5\lambda}\left[\dfrac{\lambda}{8}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)+\dfrac{25\lambda}{72}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,\tilde{\ell}+2)\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{64\lambda}{12-5\lambda}\dfrac{12}{12-5\lambda}\left[\dfrac{\lambda}{8}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)+\dfrac{25\lambda}{72}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+3)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,\tilde{\ell}+3)\right]

\displaystyle-\dfrac{1368-525\lambda}{2(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell})+\left[\dfrac{19\lambda}{2(12-5\lambda)}+\dfrac{57\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\right]v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)+\dfrac{200}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{4\lambda}{3(12-5\lambda)}\Bigg[\dfrac{8\lambda}{9-4\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,\tilde{\ell}+1)+\left[\dfrac{55\lambda}{9-4\lambda}-\dfrac{20\lambda}{12-5\lambda}\right]v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,\tilde{\ell}+2)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{64\lambda}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,\tilde{\ell}+3)-\dfrac{1368-525\lambda}{2(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)

\displaystyle+\left[\dfrac{19\lambda}{2(12-5\lambda)}+\dfrac{57\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\right]v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)+\dfrac{200}{12-5\lambda}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+3)\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell})+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+1)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+2)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{\ell}+3),

for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. The induction hypothesis now implies that it suffices to show that

\displaystyle-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,m)+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,m+1)

(135)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,m+2)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N,m+3)\geq 0,

for all
m=2,\ldots,N-6
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and for
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. We prove this statement by yet another induction argument. First of all, consider the case
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
. We start by verifying the correctness of expression (
135
) for
m=N-6
. Now, assume that it is satisfied for
m=\tilde{m}+1
for some
\tilde{m}=2,\ldots,N-7
. Using expression (
96
) and this induction hypothesis, we now obtain

\displaystyle-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m})+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+1)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{2\lambda}{3-\lambda}\Bigg[-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+1)+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{1}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)\Bigg]\geq 0.

Hence, expression (
135
) holds for all
m=2,\ldots,N-6
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
.

We proceed to consider the case
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Again using Table
14
, we verify the validity of expression (
135
) for
m=N-6
and
m=N-7
. Now, we suppose that it is true for all
m=\tilde{m}+1,\ldots,N-6
for some
\tilde{m}=2,\ldots,N-8
. By expression (
97
), we have

\displaystyle-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m})+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+1)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}\left[\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+1)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}\left[\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}\left[\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)\right]

\displaystyle-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}\left[\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+5)\right]

\displaystyle=\dfrac{7\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}\Bigg[-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+1)+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-5\lambda)}\Bigg[-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+4)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+5)\Bigg],

for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Invoking the induction hypothesis, we can now conclude that

\displaystyle-\dfrac{3(4104-3399\lambda+692\lambda^{2})}{2(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m})+\dfrac{\lambda(8721-3067\lambda)}{6(9-4\lambda)(12-5\lambda)}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+1)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{10800-4800\lambda+919\lambda^{2}}{648-558\lambda+120\lambda^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+2)-\dfrac{800\lambda(1-\lambda)}{3(12-5\lambda)^{2}}v^{\pi_{2}}_{\lambda}(N,\tilde{m}+3)\geq 0,

for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Thus, expression (
135
) holds for all
m=2,\ldots,N-6
for
k=2
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
as well. Hence, expression (
134
) is satisfied for all
\ell=2,\ldots,N-5
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and for
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
, which in turn implies the validity of inequality (
120
) for all
j=2,\ldots,N-4
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and for
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
.

We finalize the proof by showing the validity of inequalities (
121
) and (
123
). The correctness of inequalities (
122
) and (
124
) then follow immediately from symmetry. In the proofs of inequalities (
121
) and (
123
), we make use of the more involved induction argument outlined in the main part of the proof of Theorem
3.1
.

Inequality (
121
)

•

Induction base:
We show that inequality (
121
) holds for states
(i,N-4)
,
(N-4,j)
,
i,j=2,\ldots,N-4
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. To this end, we use an embedded induction argument over the length of the shortest side of the rectangle. By symmetry, it suffices to handle the case
(N-4,j)
,
j=2,\ldots,N-4
. First of all, we verify the validity of the inequality for state
(N-4,N-4)
using Table
14
. Now, suppose that it is true for state
(N-4,n+1)
for some
n=2,\ldots,N-5
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. By expression (
102
), we have

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{5\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+2)\Big),

for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Through rearranging, we obtain

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+2)\Big),

for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. The induction hypothesis and inequality (
117
) now yield,

v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)>0,

for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Hence, inequality (
121
) holds for all states
(N-4,j)
,
j=2,\ldots,N-4
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
.

•

Induction hypothesis
: Suppose that inequality (
121
) holds for states
(i,n+1)
,
(n+1,i)
for all
i=2,\ldots,n+1
for some
n=2,\ldots,N-5
. We refer to this induction hypothesis as the
global induction hypothesis
.

•

Induction step
: First of all, we show that the induction hypothesis implies the validity of inequality (
121
) for state
(n,n)
. Using expression (
102
), we obtain

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{5\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+2)\Big)>0,

for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
by the induction hypothesis, establishing the correctness of inequality (
121
) for state
(n,n)
.

Now, in addition to the global induction hypothesis, we make the assumption that inequality (
121
) holds for some state
(n,\ell+1)
, where
\ell=2,\ldots,n-1
. We show that, under the global and additional induction hypotheses, the validity of inequality (
121
) for state
(n,\ell+1)
carries over to state
(n,\ell)
. This implies the correctness of inequality (
121
) for all states
(i,n)
,
(n,i)
,
i=2,\ldots,n
under the global induction hypothesis. Using expression (
102
), we obtain

\displaystyle v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{5\lambda}{3(9-4\lambda)}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+2)\Big)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+2)\Big)>0,

by the global and additional induction hypotheses. It follows that inequality (
121
) is satisfied for states
(n,i)
,
(i,n)
for all
i=2,\ldots,n
under the global induction hypothesis. From the full induction argument, we can now conclude that inequality (
121
) holds for all states
(i,j)
,
i,j=2,\ldots,N-4
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
.

Inequality (
123
)

•

Induction base:
We prove that inequality (
123
) is valid for states
(i,N-4)
,
(N-4,j)
,
i,j=2,\ldots,N-4
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
, again by means of an embedded induction argument over the length of the shortest side of the rectangle. By symmetry, it is sufficient to prove the statement for states
(N-4,j)
,
j=2,\ldots,N-4
. First, we use Table
14
to verify the correctness of inequality (
123
) for state
(N-4,N-4)
. Now, assume that it holds for state
(N-4,n+1)
for some
n=2,\ldots,N-5
, for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Expression (
102
) yields

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{4\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{9\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{5\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+3)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+3)\Big).

Through rearranging, we obtain

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)

\displaystyle-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)\Big)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)

\displaystyle+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+3)\Big)+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+1)

\displaystyle-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+2)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-2,n+2)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+3)\Big).

From the induction hypothesis and inequality (
118
), it now follows that

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-3,n+1)

\displaystyle-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(N-4,n+2)>0.

Thus, inequality (
123
) holds for all states
(N-4,j)
,
j=2,\ldots,N-4
for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
.

•

Induction hypothesis:
Suppose that inequality (
123
) is valid for states
(i,n+1)
,
(n+1,j)
for all
i,j=2,\ldots,n+1
for some
n=2,\ldots,N-5.
We refer to this induction hypothesis as the
global induction hypothesis.

•

Induction step:
First of all, we show that the induction hypothesis implies the correctness of inequality (
123
) for state
(n,n)
. By expression (
102
), we have

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{4\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{9\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+2)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{5\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+3)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+3)\Big).

Rearranging yields

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)

\displaystyle-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)\Big)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)

\displaystyle-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+3)\Big)+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)

\displaystyle+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+1)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+2)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,n+2)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+3)\Big).

Invoking the induction hypothesis three times, we now obtain

-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,n+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,n+2)>0,

for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
. Hence, inequality (
123
) is satisfied for state
(n,n)
.

We proceed to make the assumption that inequality (
123
) holds for some state
(n,\ell+1)
, where
\ell=2,\ldots,n-1
, in addition to the global induction hypothesis. We show that the global and additional induction hypotheses imply the validity of inequality (
123
) for state
(n,\ell)
. Invoking expression (
102
), we obtain

\displaystyle-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+2)

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{4\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)\Big)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{9\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+2)\Big)

\displaystyle-\dfrac{5\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)+v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+3)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+3)\Big)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)

\displaystyle-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)\Big)+\dfrac{\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+2)

\displaystyle+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+3)\Big)+\dfrac{3\lambda}{9-4\lambda}\Big(-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)

\displaystyle+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+1)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+2)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+2,\ell+2)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+3)\Big).

It now follows from the global and additional induction hypotheses that

-3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell)+3v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell)-4v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+1)+9v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n+1,\ell+1)-5v^{\pi_{k}}_{\lambda}(n,\ell+2)>0.

Thus, inequality (
123
) holds for all states
(n,i)
,
(i,n)
,
i=2,\ldots,n
under the global induction hypothesis. The full induction argument now implies that inequality (
123
) is valid for all states
(i,j)
,
i,j=2,\ldots,N-4
for
k=1
,
\lambda\in[\lambda_{c},1)
and
k=2
,
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{c}]
.
